Adoption Hub
Success starts here
Launch, train, and grow with these helpful resources.
  * Change management
  * Onboard & train users
  * Roll out new features
  * Get insights & optimize


### A framework for success
A successful rollout starts with leadership buy-in, sharing relevant examples of how the tools help daily work, and monitoring adoption to provide support where teams need it most.
[ Start planning ](https://explore.webex.com/adoption-methodology)
![A woman wearing a headset, interacting with a Cisco desk device during a video conference.](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/adopt/adoption_methodology.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)
### Friction is normal—here's how to handle it
Progress stalls when awareness is low, relevance is unclear, and leadership visibility drops. Create momentum by sharing practical strategies with relatable use cases or get creative with gamification.
[ Get inspired ](https://explore.webex.com/overcome-hurdles)
![Two colleagues collaborating in a meeting room, using a large interactive display for a brainstorming session.](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/adopt/get_inspired.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)
### Track progress and create your success story
As your rollout progresses, check in on how you're tracking toward your goals. Use built-in tools to monitor usage and business outcomes, and capture success stories along the way.
[ See how ](https://explore.webex.com/measure-success)
![A team in a conference room participating in a hybrid meeting, with a large screen displaying business analytics.](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/adopt/share_success.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)

Create a moderator-only space in the Webex App to share announcements, best practices, and new features—making resources easily accessible where users are already working.
[ See more tips ](https://explore.webex.com/boost-awareness)

Webex Insider
#  Inspired by and built for Webex customers.
Connect with people who share your passion for Webex and gain inside access to the new concepts from the Webex portfolio product and experience teams.
[Register now ](https://www.webex.com/insider#registernow-anchor) [ Watch Video ](javascript:;)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/dg/insider/webex-insider-hero.jpg)
Explore Webex Insider programs
Our suite of programs, driven by Cisco Insider, empowers collaboration enthusiasts to connect, learn from industry leaders, and contribute to Webex's evolution in a dynamic environment.
  * Advocacy
  * User Group
  * User Research
  * Champions


### Build your peer network and get rewarded for your engagement.
**Former name:** Webex Ambassadors
**What to expect:** Join a loyalty and rewards program for Webex customers to earn points for completing activities of their choice and redeem them for exclusive rewards.
*Partners can participate but aren't eligible for rewards.
**What you’ll gain:** Get Webex updates, boost your professional visibility and connections, and gain recognition as a Webex authority and influencer.
[ Register for Advocates ](https://insideradvocates.cisco.com/join/advocates)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/dg/insider/webex-insider-advocate.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)
### Get direct access to product and engineering teams.
**Former name:** Webex Experts
**What to expect:** Engage with your peers plus Webex product teams, engineering teams, and GTM teams to learn best practices and share your experiences using Webex solutions.
Provide feedback to product teams and help shape the future of Webex.
**What you’ll gain:** Gain access to exclusive content via technical briefings, roadmap sessions, and live demos.
[ Apply for User Group ](https://community.cisco.com/t5/cisco-insider-user-group/ct-p/ccp-home)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/dg/insider/webex-insider-usergroup.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)
### Form the future of Webex in collaboration with our user experience experts.
**What to expect:** Contribute to Webex's development and enhancement by registering for our research panel and participating in studies tailored to your role, product use, and business requirements.
**What you’ll gain:** Influence product roadmaps by voicing your requirements and offering feedback directly to the Product and Development teams. You'll also enjoy occasional behind-the-scenes looks at product development and creation.
[ Sign up for User Research studies ](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/dg/insider/webex-insider-userresearch.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg)
### Grow your professional network and influence within the Webex/Collab community.
**What to expect:** Discuss the future of tech, gain early insights into Webex’s new announcements, and interact with experts via beta tests, feedback sessions, pre-briefings, and opportunities to host Cisco Champion Radio.
**What you’ll gain:** Gain early access to upcoming Cisco innovations, connect with top engineers, grow your tech network, improve your blogging and podcasting talent via Creators Club and Cisco Champion Radio, contribute feedback through various testing and group formats, and enjoy special experiences at Cisco Live events.

FAQs
Find answers to commonly asked questions so you can connect with the programs that matter you to most
  * Webex Insider
  * Advocacy
  * User Group
  * User Research


Is the Webex Insider program part of the Cisco Insider Program? 
Yes it is! The Webex Insider program is powered by Cisco Insider. Click here to learn more about the [Cisco Insider program](https://www.cisco.com/c/en/us/about/cisco-insider.html). 
Do I need to register again if I am already part of the Cisco Insider Program? 
For the Webex Advocacy and User Group programs you will not need to register again. For joining User Research please complete this brief [registration form](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq).
What is the Webex Insider Advocates program? 
The Webex Insider Advocates program was formerly known as the Webex Ambassadors program. We have rebranded and relaunched this program as a loyalty and rewards program for Webex customers to earn points for completing activities of their choice (share social posts, complete trainings & certifications, participate in case studies, user research, etc.) and redeem exclusive rewards. We’ve partnered with Influitive to host the program on this platform, which you may be familiar with if you’re a Cisco Insider Advocate too. Join the program [here](https://insideradvocates.cisco.com/forum/).
What are the perks of being a Webex Insider Advocate? 
There are intangible and tangible benefits to being a Webex Insider Advocate. You'll have a one stop shop central location to get the latest and greatest Webex news such as a new product release and upcoming events. You'll also have the opportunity to connect with your peers in the industry to share your knowledge and learn from them. You can also cash in points for exclusive rewards available only to members like you.
Does the Webex Ambassadors program still exist? 
The Webex Ambassadors program has folded into the Webex Insider Advocates program. Prior Webex Ambassadors have automatically been added to the Webex Insider Advocates program if they are still a current Webex customer. The Webex Insider Advocate program can be accessed [here](https://insideradvocates.cisco.com/forum/).
I was in the process of taking the Webex Ambassador courses on GoLearn, how will I earn my badge(s)? 
Webex Ambassador courses on Webex Academy (formerly GoLearn) have been retired and we've launched the Webex Insider Advocates program to replace it. The new program includes fun challenges and opportunities to earn points that can be used to redeem rewards. Join the Webex Insider Advocates program [here](https://insideradvocates.cisco.com/forum/).
Can I invite my colleagues to join Webex Insider Advocates? 
Yes! We welcome all Webex customers to join the Webex Insider Advocates program. Our only requirements are that they be a current customer, are using Webex as an IT admin, and are advocates for Webex. Invite your colleagues to join the program [here](https://insideradvocates.cisco.com/forum/).
What is the Webex Insider User Group program and why should I join? 
The Webex Insider User Group enables you to engage with your peers plus Webex product teams, engineering teams and marketing teams to learn best practices and share real-life experiences using Webex solutions.
By joining the program, you’ll gain:
  * Direct access to product owners, subject matter experts and technical briefings
  * An opportunity to provide product feedback
  * Curated content and insight into the product roadmap
  * The ability to share your experiences and learn from your peers in an open forum


Does the Webex Experts program still exist? 
The Webex Experts program has folded into the Webex Insider User Group program. The Webex Insider User Group program can be accessed [here](https://community.cisco.com/t5/customer-connection/ct-p/ccp-home).
If I was already a Webex Expert do I need to re-enroll? 
No. Now that we are part of the Cisco Insider program your access will be maintained. 
What is the Webex Insider User Research? 
[Webex Insider User Research](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq) is a program through which you can actively participate in shaping the design and development of Webex products. Join our panel of collaboration users, partners, admins, and developers to directly influence our product direction. 
What do I get from the program? Why should I join? 
[By joining](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq) you will be added to our close contact list of forward thinkers and trailblazers. As a member, you'll have opportunities to participate in user studies, provide your feedback for launched products you already use, others that are still just a concept. You will get to share your wishes and ideas, as well as get an early glimpse into what we are working on and what is coming next for Webex!
What will be required from me? How often am I going to hear from you? 
Opting in to join our panel is easy and voluntary ([this survey](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq) will take ~2 minutes). Your contact details and product-interests will be used for Product / Research purposes only. We won't overwhelm your inbox: depending on our product roadmap and your interests and usage, you will be contacted up to 2-3 times per calendar year regarding participation in Cisco Webex Research. This includes invitations to participate in user studies, surveys, or opportunities to provide feedback. If you are not interested – you can ignore, ask to postpone, or withdraw from the program at any time simply by replying to our email.
What products are you covering? How can I participate only in studies and activities that are relevant and interesting to me? 
Our Webex Insider Research Program expands and stretches to cover anything Webex and collaboration: From AI/VR, meeting and messaging experiences and integrations, Contact Center, Control Hub and partner Hub, Calling, and everything in between (think: Migration to cloud! Interoperability! Devices and Smart Spaces experiences!). 
Tell us what you are curious or passionate about with Webex [when you join](https://ciscochannels.qualtrics.com/jfe/form/SV_9Zj1nm0wk6XsWuq) or update your choices later.
More Questions? 
Contact us at webexuxr@cisco.com.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# Easy screen sharing in 2021 with Webex.
## Enjoy video-first meetings with integrated voice, video and screen sharing, that lets you collaborate more effectively with customers, partners, and employees--without the need to travel.
[ Start For Free ](https://cart.webex.com/sign-up)
## Do I have control over what I screen share?
With [Webex Meetings](https://www.webex.com/content/webex/c/en_US/index/video-conferencing.html) you can choose exactly what you want to   
share—no more, no less.
  * Share individual files or documents, such as a specific Word doc or PowerPoint.
  * Share entire application sets, such as productivity tools or web browsers.
  * Share multimedia viewers and players so you can show video or animation in a Webex Meeting at 30 frames per second.
  * Share your entire screen, including everything you have open and in view.


The choice is yours. You have complete control over   
what and when you share.
### Learn more about [screen sharing best practices](https://blog.webex.com/screen-share/best-screen-sharing-practices-for-small-businesses).
## Can I share screens from any device?
With Webex, you can share an application or your entire screen with remote attendees in real-time across any device-- even when you’re on the go. You can share your content or screen from your:
  * Browser.
  * Android devices.
  * iPhone or iPad devices. —You can even share 3D models virtually in your meetings from iOS devices!
  * Cisco Webex Device. Screenshare wirelessly to a Webex Device via Proximity. You can also share whiteboard sessions into a Webex meeting from a Webex Board.


[Start for Free](https://cart.webex.com/sign-up).
## Can anyone in my meeting share a screen? 
### Screen sharing isn’t limited to just the host. Your attendees can take control and share content as well. Best of all, you can collaborate together, in real-time, to keep your work moving forward.
  * Open a document and edit or mark-up the file together.
  * Start a whiteboard and draw your ideas, for all to see. You can use both text and graphic tools to whiteboard together as a group.


## How do I share my screen?
  1. Select Share in the control bar on your screen.
  2. Select to share your screen or application from the list of open applications.
     * To share an application that isn’t open, select Share, and Other Applications.
  3. To start a whiteboard session, select Share, and New Whiteboard.
  4. To stop screen sharing, select Stop Sharing on the floating bar at the top of the screen.
  5. You can also screenshare from your Webex Meetings desktop app when connected to a Webex device.


### Learn more on how to [share your screen](https://help.webex.com/en-us/5ddww5/Share-Content-in-Cisco-Webex-Meetings-and-Cisco-Webex-Events).

## Consent Manager

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

Startups
## One app to get your work done.
As an entrepreneur, you’re focused on bringing new innovation to market—fast. Empower seamless, efficient collaboration with one easy-to-use, secure app to call, meet, and message. With Webex, your startup can build stronger relationships, reach more customers, and hire the best talent, anywhere.

Skip the learning curve and get started in minutes. Sign up for Webex, for free.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# All-in-one, AI-powered collaboration.
Connect, collaborate, and engage in one place. Eight powerful workflows elevate teamwork and maximize productivity, for every workstyle.

## Maximize value. Minimize costs.
Elevate teamwork with all the collaboration tools you need, in one place.
[Contact Us ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/dg/contact-sales.html) [See Pricing Plans ](https://pricing.webex.com/us/en/hybrid-work/suite/)
Calling Stay connected with modern business phone system and flexible calling options.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/enterprise-cloud-calling.html)
Meetings Drive more productive meetings with AI-powered experiences and high-quality audio and video.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/meetings.html)
MessagingCollaborate async with rich messaging, secure file sharing, and digital whiteboarding to keep work flowing between meetings.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/suite/messaging.html)
Polling Enable active participation in every meeting with quizzes, polling, and interactive Q&A. 
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/audience-engagement.html)
Webinars Host high quality webinars and drive engagement with immersive content and interactive audience experiences. 
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/webinar.html)
Events Power virtual, in-person, and hybrid events with immersive multi-track agendas, ticketing, attendee networking, analytics, and more. 
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/events.html)
Whiteboarding Unleash creativity with a digital whiteboarding solution for everyone, on any device.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/suite/whiteboard.html)
Video Messaging  Record, share ideas, engage, and facilitate real-time feedback with intuitive video messaging.
[Learn More ](https://vidcast.io/)
## AI is now part of your team.
The AI Assistant powers experiences across the Webex Suite, from meetings and messaging, to polling and even Control Hub.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.ai/ai-assistant-for-suite-devices-cx.html?tab=tab-suite)
Never miss a moment Get caught up in a flash with meeting and messaging summaries and catch-me-up features for when you need to step away.
Put your best foot forward Leverage AI to translate messages or help you quickly change the tone of your message before sending.
Streamline management Increase IT productivity with quick answers and lightning-fast configuration changes by simply asking “Help me do...”.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# Webex   
Webinars 
(formerly Webex Events)
##  A scalable webinar platform for interactive experiences.
[Plans & Pricing ](https://pricing.webex.com/us/en/hybrid-work/webinars/?utm_medium=website&utm_source=wdc&utm_campaign=n/a&utm_content=hero&team=wdc) [Watch Video ](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=z-svwHeTWMU)
![Person joins a large gathering through Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-hero.jpg)
What makes a truly great webinar?
See this checklist for everything to consider when planning your next event.
[Get Guide ](https://www.webex.com/gp/webinar-best-practices.html)
## Global reach.   
Impactful experiences.
![An executive reaches a large audience with Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_1.png)
![Example of Webex Events user interface](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_2.png)
![Person makes connections with Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_3.png)
* ![An executive reaches a large audience with Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_1.png)
###### Reach global audiences at scale
Accommodate larger audiences than ever before and reach more corners of the world.
* ![Example of Webex Events user interface](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_2.png)
###### Deliver virtual webinars with confidence
Practice before presenting, simplify webinar management, and set the stage for success.
* ![Person makes connections with Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/webinar-overview_3.png)
###### Create meaningful connections
Spark engaging conversations and memorable moments.
## Simplify webinars without compromising experience. 
Create engaging experiences for everyone, from small sessions to massive global audiences.
Reach everyone Accommodate up to 100,000 attendees and reach global audiences with real-time translations in 100+ languages.
Bring your brand identity Curate the attendee registration experience with custom branding options and themes.
Practice to perfection Rehearse the entire presentation with your co-panelists before you go live on stage.
Present like a pro Bring your presentations to life with immersive share by placing yourself directly on the content you share.
Engage the audience Encourage active participation with moderated Q&A, live polling, chat, emoji reactions, and gesture recognition.
Direct the show Manage the stage and content that your audience sees before, during, and after the event.
Make it accessible Let attendees listen to the event in their preferred language with live interpreters included in session.
Discuss in small groups Dive deeper into topics or get to know panelists more intimately with breakout sessions.
Integrates with your favorite apps Easily integrate with tools you use everyday including Miro, and Slido.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

The future of events is here.
Event professionals trust Webex Events to power their event programs end-to-end, on a single platform.
[ Watch a Demo ](javascript:;)
End-to-End Management
Powerful event software and services give event professionals and marketers the tools to manage events across the entire event lifecycle.
Flexibility Built-In
Easily switch from in-person to virtual formats, or bring both audiences together with a hybrid event.
Better Results
Show proven ROI from every event with detailed data for attendee engagement, ticket sales, sponsorship, and more.
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/mad-fest-banner.jpg)
Learn how MAD//Fest Hosted an In-person, Virtual, and Hybrid Event, All on Webex Events
[ Read Case Study ](https://socio.events/resources/case-studies/mad-fest-case-study)
Intuitive event tech for seamless experiences 
Webex Events provides everything you need to elevate the event experience, whether your audience joins in-person or virtually.
[ Watch a Demo ](javascript:;)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/01-registration.jpg)
Registration & Ticketing 
Build fully branded registration in minutes. Flexible ticketing enables multiple ticket types, prices, groups, discount codes, and more – all with instant payouts.
[ Explore Registration ](https://socio.events/products/online-event-registration-software)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/02-mobile-event-app.jpg)
Mobile Event App 
Allow your attendees to access your event content in-person or on the go from an intuitive and fully customizable event app.
[ Explore the Mobile Event App ](https://socio.events/products/mobile-event-app)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/03-on-site.jpg)
On-site Solutions 
Power seamless in-person event check-in and badge printing. And with more onsite solutions like lead retrieval and live display, it’s easy to deliver connected attendee experiences.
[ Explore Onsite Check-in ](https://socio.events/products/event-check-in-app)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/04-production-studio.jpg)
Production Studio 
Create professional, fully branded live streams that are tailored to your audience with no need for downloads or outside software.
[ Explore the Production Studio ](https://socio.events/products/live-streaming-software)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/05-virtual-event-platform.jpg)
Virtual Event Platform 
Host engaging, interactive virtual events that attendees love. Build multi-track agendas with immersive content, streaming, and attendee networking to drive active participation.
[ Explore the Virtual Event Platform ](https://socio.events/products/virtual-event-platform)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/suite/event/06-lead-retrieval.jpg)
Lead Retrieval 
Lead Retrieval is the most seamless way for your sponsors and exhibitors to capture leads with enriched data – all by scanning attendees’ QR codes.
[ Explore Lead Retrieval ](https://socio.events/products/lead-retrieval-app)
Webex Events comes standard with all the features you need.

Still have questions about Webex Events?
What's the difference between Webex Events and Webex Webinars?
Webex Webinars is the ideal solution for intuitive, engaging webinars that scale with your business. If you're looking for an all-in-one event platform to power virtual, in-person and hybrid events–from multi-track conferences to tradeshows to networking events, Webex Events is the solution for you!
I'm a Webex Suite Enterprise Agreement customer. Do I have access to Webex Events?
Webex Events is included in A-FLEX-EA-SUITE, A-FLEX-EA-P-SUITE, A-FLEX-EA-MUL, A-FLEX-EA-MCL, E3C-WEBEX-SUITE, and E3H-WEBEX-SUITE plans. This list continues to expand, so check back for updates! To learn more about Webex Events and how to access as part of your plan, please contact your Webex Account Manager.
Can I purchase Webex Events separate from the Webex Suite?
No, Webex Events is exclusively offered as a part of select Webex Suite Enterprise Agreements. If you're a Suite Enterprise customer, contact your Webex Account Manager to see if Webex Events is a part of your plan!

## Energize your   
audience to join   
the conversation.

## Less monologue, more interaction.
![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Create-meaningful-engagement.jpg)
![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Foster-an-inclusive-culture.jpg)
![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Build-stronger-connections.jpg)
* ![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Create-meaningful-engagement.jpg)
###### Create meaningful engagement
Interact with participants at scale and ensure that everyone's engaged.
* ![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Foster-an-inclusive-culture.jpg)
###### Foster an inclusive culture
Democratize discussions, empower everyone, and collect your audience's feedback to improve decision-making.
* ![User has an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Build-stronger-connections.jpg)
###### Build stronger connections
Facilitate team bonding, create transparency, and build trust between leaders and employees.
## Engage your audience with live polling.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/documents/pdf/CM-2147_Slido_AAG.pdf)
Give them options Use a multiple-choice poll to capture everyone's thoughts quickly.
Or, ask them to write their own Type in custom answers or comments with open-text feedback.
Try a pop quiz Test their knowledge live with a timer and leaderboard.
Get instant feedback Get a feel for how they liked the session and topics.
Visualize keywords Display audience answers in a word cloud to see what's most popular.
Democratize prioritization Let the audience rank options based on their preferences.
## Create two-way dialogue with live Q&A.
* ![Example of a Webex Meeting with live Q&A](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Collect-questions-live.jpg)
###### Collect questions live
Participants can send in questions directly during the meeting.
* ![User listens in on a Webex Meeting Q&A session](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Moderate-Q-A-in-real-time.jpg)
###### Moderate Q&A in real time
Review incoming questions and filter to focus on relevant topics.
* ![user organizes questions with labels](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Organize-queries-with-labels.jpg)
###### Organize queries with labels
Categorize and address questions by topics to keep things efficient.
* ![User addresses questions in a Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Address-the-most-critical-questions.jpg)
###### Address the most critical questions
Let the audience upvote questions to ensure the most important concerns are addressed first.
* ![Person responds in an interactive Webex Meeting](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/audience-engagement/Offer-more-ways-to-respond.jpg)
###### Offer more ways to respond
Reply to submitted questions as a host, then let participants comment on each other's questions.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# Webex   
Whiteboard
## Unleash creativity with digital whiteboarding.
Bring teams together to co-create, annotate, ideate, and solve problems with an easy-to-use whiteboard.
[Start for Free ](https://whiteboard.webex.com/)[Contact Us ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/dg/contact-sales.html)
![People collaborate in real time with Webex whiteboarding, adding sticky notes, a flow chart, etc. to the digital whiteboard.](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-hero.png)
## Intuitive whiteboarding on any device.
![A professional collaborates on a digital whiteboard, editing it from his mobile phone.](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-video.png) [ ](javascript:;)
## Brainstorm, ideate, and co-create with anyone, anywhere.
Create together in real time or pick up after the meeting with a limitless whiteboard that's always available.
[Learn More ](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/documents/pdf/digital-whiteboarding-with-webex.pdf)
Experience frictionless whiteboarding Quickly express, show, and capture ideas on a whiteboard that never runs out of space.
Ensure everyone’s included Bring everyone together for an equally rich collaboration experience, regardless of location or devices.
Keep your ideas secure Secure by design, private by default, Webex defends against threats no matter where you’re working.
## Digital whiteboarding, reimagined.
Ideation, annotation, and co-creation for the entire team, made simple.
[Start for Free ](https://whiteboard.webex.com/)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-feature1.jpg)

###### Collaborate your way
Draw, write, type, and add stickies or emojis to express your ideas the way you want.
* ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-feature2.jpg)
###### Spend time creating, less time getting started
Get teams and external collaborators up and running quickly from any location.
* ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-feature3.jpg)
###### Access from anywhere, on any device, at any time
Collaborate from your phone, laptop, tablet, or Webex endpoint with a tailored experience for each device.
* ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/wbx/us/images/rebrand/new/whiteboard-feature4.jpg)
###### Create securely as a team
Co-create with the comfort of a secure whiteboarding solution featuring end-to-end encryption.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

The future of CX is automated, personalized, and intelligent. 
Embed automation, personalization, and contextual insights throughout the customer journey—all while unlocking actionable data.
* ####  Intelligent front door 
Autonomously resolve customer requests across any voice and digital channel or multi-modal experience with proactive messaging and AI agents.
* ####  Empowered workforce 
Enable agents to resolve queries efficiently with intelligent routing, AI-powered assistance, and real-time guidance, while giving CX leaders the insights to enhance team performance and deliver trusted CX.
* ####  Data-driven insights 
Aggregate and unify complex data across every touchpoint in real time to deliver contextual insights and accelerate decision-making.
The AI-native platform for secure, scalable CX.
Webex Customer Experience solutions combine the industry-leading innovation you want with the security and scalability you need to deliver trusted CX
AI-native 
Interoperability 
Integrations 
Unified data 
Security 
###  AI across the entire platform. 
AI isn’t just a feature, it’s embedded across the platform to deliver smarter insights, proactive support, and intelligent automation to enhance customer experiences, supercharge productivity, and drive operational excellence.
###  Seamless interoperability. 
No need to rip and replace, Webex Customer Experience solutions work with your on-premises systems or across diverse third-party solutions.
###  Rapid integration to your business apps. 
Connect to your essential business applications with our extensive library of pre-built integrations or deploy bespoke integrations tailored to your unique needs.
###  Unified data layer from multiple sources. 
Leverage a unified data layer to ingest inputs from multiple sources, enabling real-time profiling, predictive intelligence, and the ability to take context aware actions.
###  Trusted and secure by design. 
Your data stays protected by design with AI that operates from strict guardrails to ensure privacy, responsibility, control, all backed by Cisco to help ensure peace of mind with every interaction.

## Real world. Real success.
See how customers are reimagining CX with Webex Customer Experience solutions.
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-dbschenker.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-dbschenker.svg)
With over 2,000 agents facing over 250,000 calls per month, DB Schenker needed a modern cloud contact center solution to improve agility and streamline customer service. Webex provided the intelligent cloud-based solution they needed to transform their operations.
[ Read Case Study ](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/how-webex-helped-db-schenker-enhance-its-operational-agility/)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-valeris.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-valeris.svg)
Valeris, a trusted life sciences commercialization partner, was seeking an intelligent solution to elevate the experience for agents and customers. With Webex, they were able to simplify processes, integrate channels, and deliver AI-powered assistance and self-service to enhance every engagement.
[ Read Case Study ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/mercalis.html)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-medstar.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-medstar.svg)

Handling 15,000 calls a day, MedStar needed a contact center that could keep up. With Webex, they cut average handle time, scaled to 10,000+ weekly appointments, and gave agents the flexibility to work from anywhere, all without compromising patient access.
[ See How They Did It ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/medstar-health.html)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-aids-heathcare-foundation.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-ahf.svg)
Scaling from $3B to $8B in three years across 48 countries, AHF needed a platform that could grow with them. Webex replaced fragmented legacy systems with a unified cloud solution, streamlining patient communications and giving IT a single pane of glass to manage it all.
[ Read the Full Story ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/aids-healthcare-foundation.html)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-maersk.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-maersk.svg)
Maersk strategically embraced Webex to modernize its contact center, empowering 15,000 agents across over 120 countries with a unified, agile, and AI-ready cloud solution that resulted in significant operational efficiencies and enhanced customer experiences.
[ Read Case Study ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/maersk.html)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/2026/new-yum-brands.jpg)
![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/products/customer-experience/customer-stories/logo-yum.svg)
Running KFC, Taco Bell, and Pizza Hut across 59,000+ locations in 155 countries means complex support at massive scale. Yum! Brands replaced legacy systems with Webex Contact Center giving agents more time for high-value work and the enterprise cloud agility to match.
[ See How They Scaled ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/yum-brands.html)

Webex AI Agent
Dynamic AI interactions that lead to customer resolutions.
[ Watch Video ](javascript:;)
[ Request a Demo ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/ai-agent.html#demo-anchor)
Reimagine self-service.
The Webex AI Agent answers inquiries and fulfills customer intents across a wide range of channels–all while delivering personalized, conversational experiences.
Deliver human-like conversations 
The AI Agent delivers interactions that feel like conversing with a real person, while also understanding an individual's needs, remembering their history, and adapting to their preferences in real-time. 
Easily design and deploy agents 
Design an AI Agent tailored to your needs with the choice to build an autonomous agent for dynamic, natural conversations or a scripted agent that uses specific, pre-configured answers. 
Fully resolve customer issues 
Powerful integrations with your back-office systems allow agents to fulfill tasks that bring complete resolutions to customer issues. 
## Answers for every customer.
#### 
Effectively handle challenging questions with an AI Agent that can quickly understand customer needs and customize every answer.
Deploy across voice and digital channels 
Scale conversations across a wide range of channels, including Voice, SMS, Email, Livechat, Facebook Messenger, Apple Messages for Business, and Whatsapp.
Provide always-on, 24/7 service 
Provide instant support for your customers whenever they need it, whether they need to schedule an appointment, troubleshoot problems or get answers to their queries.
Handoff seamlessly to human agents 
Transfer conversations seamlessly if human assistance is needed, providing the full context so agents can pick up right where the AI Agent left off.
Scale with multi-lingual agents 
Overcome language barriers with a single AI Agent that is capable of conversing in over 10 languages.
Gain real-time insights 
Evaluate the performance of AI Agents with automatic agent testing and scoring that helps you optimize interactions and maximize customer satisfaction.
##  Agentic use cases for every industry.
Webex AI Agent autonomously resolves your highest-volume interactions to drive faster resolutions, lower operational costs, and improve CSAT—across every industry.
Financial Services
Healthcare
Retail
Travel
Routine inquiries dominate call volume for financial institutions, and every escalation drives up cost. Webex AI Agent resolves the most common requests instantly and securely.
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-60-money-color-black.svg) Debit & credit card activation
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-110-lock-unlock-color-black.svg) Secure PIN resets
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-38-transcripts-color-black.svg) Fraud explanations
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-51-user-hub-color-black.svg) Account updates & payment options


![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/ai/ai-agent/image-text-tab/financial.jpg)
Healthcare contact centers face relentless demand, with zero tolerance for errors and long hold times. Webex AI Agent handles high-volume requests with accuracy, HIPAA alignment, and 24/7 availability.
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-33-seamless-collaboration-1-color-black.svg) Triage patient & employee requests
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-64-calendar-color-black.svg) Appointment booking & rescheduling
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-17-healthcare-color-black.svg) Care coordination
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-88-fullfilment-color-black.svg) Claims management


![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/ai/ai-agent/image-text-tab/healthcare.jpg)
Retail contact centers spike unpredictably. Webex AI Agent scales instantly to absorb demand surges without adding headcount, while keeping every channel experience consistent.
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-77-basket-color-black.svg) Product discovery & recommendations
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-84-delivery-color-black.svg) Order tracking & updates
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-70-logistics-color-black.svg) Refunds & returns
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-31-retail-color-black.svg) Promotions


![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/ai/ai-agent/image-text-tab/use-case-retail.jpg)
Travel disruptions are unpredictable and can spike call volume 10x overnight. Webex AI Agent manages the surge, delivers real-time resolution in multiple languages, and reduces abandon rates.
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-64-calendar-color-black.svg) Reservations & rebooking
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-86-field-agent-color-black.svg) Hotel guest concierge
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-90-guide-color-black.svg) Travel planning & itineraries
  * ![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/icons/icons-svg/black-august/Icon-73-travel-color-black.svg) Flight disruption handling


![](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/images/ai/ai-agent/image-text-tab/use-case-travel.jpg)
Build an agent tailored to your needs.
Easy to build, fast to deploy, and seamlessly integrated with your existing systems–all backed by Cisco security.
Easy to design and deploy
Use a no-code / low-code, intuitive platform to design your AI Agents, enabling you to rapidly deploy to boost operational efficiency and accelerate time to value.
Flexible options to build agents
Create either autonomous agents which use LLMs for adaptive, human-like interactions, or scripted agents which use conversational AI with controlled, pre-configured responses that are ideal for sensitive data.
Trusted security and guardrails
Cisco’s stringent data security measures and reliable AI guardrails ensure trustworthy responses and help maintain safeguards.
